- **ſ** represents the "long s."

- **¶** represents the pilcrow (paragraph mark).
- Abbreviations are transcribed with their closest Unicode equivalents or expanded in brackets where necessary for clarity.

Note on abbreviations:
*   A tilde (˜) over a vowel indicates a following 'm' or 'n' (e.g., **sciẽce** for *science*, **grãde** for *grande*).
*   The symbol **q̃** is used for *que*.
*   The symbol **⁹** is used for the suffix *-us* (e.g., **to⁹** for *tous*, **ourithe⁹** for *Ouritheus*).
*   The symbol **⁊** is the Tironian *et*, used alongside the ampersand **&** and the full word **et**.
*   The pilcrow **¶** marks the start of new sections.
